# S2E17 — "ASHES" — Showrunner plan (**revision 1, 2026-10-30**; built on the real `saves/after-s2e16.json` and the published `episodes/s2e16.json`; MAIN TIMELINE — `hard-saying/fork/` not read and not applied; the Episode Architect added §2B on 2026-10-30 — **§2B wins on any conflict**)

> Writers' room only. 🔒 = hidden canon: plant, never surface in a cue / key / previously / logline / nextTime / choice text.
> **THE JOURNAL IS RETIRED (player, 2026-10-30).** **No `journal` array or journal card in any scene, choice, blueprint or brief of this episode.** Key lines ("Story so far") remain and carry the recap: every key a complete, spoiler-safe sentence in the player's second person.
> **S2E16 is PLAYED.** Every E16 read is **flat** on the played value (§9). The Architect still gates defensively on the real flag names (exhaustive if/else, never nested).
> **Process:** the notes session is COMPLETE (player: **"Phenomenal episode, it's finally happening! Ready for the next one, any questions?"**; brief "After S2E16" records the answers). Every downstream agent, Assembly included, runs its full step; nobody pauses for notes.
> **What this episode is:** the morning after, and the waiting. The levee spent every truth; S2E17 inherits none of nola_ledger rows 1–17. It is an episode of **ashes, goodbyes, road, withdrawal and a confession that isn't one** — quiet on the surface, the whole season pressing underneath. **Lent is the waiting.** Jack and Lena keep the levee's pact to the letter: **no touch on any path** (the next touch is S2E19's kiss, after "No."). The romance is the inch not crossed. Skye is answered warm as ever, and that is what makes it unbearable.
> **Binding inputs, in order of precedence:** brief (newest last) — **"After S2E16"** (**each tells their best friend — Jack → Theo, Lena → Laney — ONLY that they're breaking up with Skye / Julian; nothing further stated, everything implied; Theo and Laney in their own registers; the alliance's terror of jinxing it peaks; the Easter reveal stays intact** · **Skye's calls on the drive home: Jack answers warm as ever, because he does love her; she suspects nothing until the porch Tue Feb 23; he says nothing of it on the phone** · **the return confession to Osei is SATURDAY Feb 20, regular confession hours, after a night of not sleeping; before Skye; end of S2E17 or start of S2E18 per the map**) · "After S2E15" (Hale notices — S2E18, not here) · "After S2E14" (**the Broussards' confusion, binding S2E16–S2E17**: Celeste careful, Tony blunt, Mémère's French, Remy souring, Tess's look, Josie and Jude out loud; no answers) · "After S2E13" (Broussards a full ensemble; Lena at full strength; journal retired) · "After S2E12" (first "I love you" between them is S3; date #1/#2 — not here; Easter dinner and the reveal — not here) · **"Season 2 back-half decisions"** (what Jack tells Skye on the porch — S2E18; CORRECTION: the first kiss after "No." — S2E19; very slow heat) · **"After S2E11"** (**Jack's return confession: as soon as he's back, before Skye; not a sin bothering him; the plan made; the "same direction" line; he so deeply loves Skye and had honestly planned on marrying her someday — Nonna's "a good name for a wife" — that he is distraught; Osei won't tell him what to do; talking aloud helps; Osei, with a small laugh: he hasn't confessed anything; he confesses venial sins from New Orleans — no mortal sins; devotional confession explained in Osei's voice; sound theology**; honor Skye fully) · "New Orleans arc decisions" (Jack faithful throughout) · "After S2E10" (**Kevin NOT in the alliance**) · standing entries (**"Ash Wednesday is the morning after. Ashes, hangovers, 'remember you are dust'; they've agreed to go home and end things honestly. Lent is the waiting"**; **"Lent 2010 observance — First Lent: separate practices, no joint plan"**: Lena fasts every Friday the old bread-and-water way, Stations at St. Brendan's Friday evenings, gives up Facebook (Laney appalled); Jack gives up cigarettes — withdrawal, itchy hands, **an unopened pack he carries, Lena quietly moved** — and fasts Fridays too, matching her out of devotion and pride; no shared Lent plan; "Laney brings the laptop" — S2E19; "the asking" — S2E19; no procedural consent beats; everyone 18+; no sex for Jack or Lena with anyone; never mock the faith; no strawmen; Jack and Lena never die) · bible §23.2 S2E17 (+ this pass's rev. 1 note and the S2E18/S2E19 hand-off notes) / §21 "Status after S2E16" + registry notes (added this pass) / §8.1, §8.2, §8.3, §8.4, §8.5, §8.7, §10.2, §10.9, §24.7 S2E17 notes (added this pass) · room.md tic registry (#1–#252) · `period/s2e17-feb17-20-2010.md` (to be filed — §12).

## 0. DATES, SHAPE, TARGET
- **Wed Feb 17, 2010 (≈6:20 a.m. CST, the attic on Dupre Street) – Sat Feb 20 (≈5:20 p.m. PST, the front steps of St. Brendan's).** Title card: **"Wednesday, February 17 – Saturday, February 20, 2010"**.
  - **The Showrunner's call on Saturday (the task allowed either; brief: "end of S2E17 or start of S2E18 per the map"):** **S2E17 keeps Saturday; the return confession is its last movement; S2E18 opens Saturday night / Sunday Feb 21.** Why: (1) the player gave the confession its own detailed S2E17 answers — they are waiting for it; (2) "Ashes" is a complete spiritual arc only if it runs from "Remember that you are dust" to absolution — the first Saturday of Lent; (3) S2E18 already carries three of the season's biggest days (Lena and Julian Sunday — off Jack's page; Hale Monday; the porch Tuesday), and the porch needs every word it can get ("honor her fully"); (4) the brief's "after a night of not sleeping" belongs to the nights of this episode (Friday's). The bible's §23.2 S2E17/S2E18/S2E19 entries carry the change.
  - **Liturgy (Researcher confirms NAB wording; §12):** **Ash Wednesday** — Joel 2:12–18 ("Even now, says the LORD, return to me with your whole heart… Rend your hearts, not your garments") · Ps 51 · 2 Cor 5:20–6:2 ("now is a very acceptable time") · **Mt 6:1–6, 16–18** ("when you fast, anoint your head and wash your face, so that you may not appear to be fasting"). The distribution formula as in the 2010 US Sacramentary [R]: "Remember, man, you are dust, and to dust you will return" (or "Turn away from sin and be faithful to the Gospel"). **Thu** — Deut 30:15–20 ("Choose life") / Luke 9:22–25 ("take up his cross daily") · **Fri** — Isa 58:1–9a / **Mt 9:14–15** ("The days will come when the bridegroom is taken away from them, and then they will fast.") · **Sat** — Isa 58:9b–14 / **Luke 5:27–32** (the call of Levi: "Those who are healthy do not need a physician, but the sick do. I have not come to call the righteous to repentance but sinners."). **None of these is preached at us**; at most a clause in Jack's head, never underlined. **Fast and abstinence (USCCB, 2010):** Ash Wednesday fast (18–59: one full meal, two smaller) and abstinence (14+); Fridays of Lent abstinence. Jack (18) and Lena (18) are bound; Mémère (88) is not and fasts anyway; Theo (not practicing), Laney (lapsed Southern Baptist) and Margot (atheist) are not bound and are each funny about it.
  - **Jack's penance (Osei, S2E11 [Candor], played): Mark 6:7–13 "every morning until you are home."** Wed = the **thirteenth** morning (the attic; "shake the dust off your feet" — Mark 6:11 — on Ash Wednesday, one clause); Thu = the **fourteenth** (Fort Stockton at dawn; Margot's Gideon); **Fri: he's home** (412) — the choice (`s2e17_home`); Saturday Osei asks.
  - **The clock home ("Thirty-five hours, the other way" — S2E16 nextTime):** depart N. Dupre Street **≈3:15 p.m. CST Wed** (Celeste will not let the van go before the twins are home from school at 2:30) → I-10 west: Baton Rouge (≈4:10), **the Atchafalaya Basin bridge at dusk (≈5:40)**, Lafayette, Lake Charles, **Houston gas (≈9:15)**, San Antonio (≈12:30 a.m.), Junction, **Sonora (≈2:35 a.m.)**, Ozona, **Fort Stockton at sunrise (≈7:40 CST)**, Van Horn, the clocks to Mountain past Van Horn (Kevin logs it), **El Paso (≈10:00 MST)**, Las Cruces, Lordsburg, the Arizona line, **Texas Canyon rest area (≈2:00 p.m.)**, Tucson, Phoenix's Broadway Curve at rush hour (≈4:45), Quartzsite, **the Colorado River at Blythe — the clocks to Pacific, the California agricultural inspection station, a Chevron (≈6:00 p.m. PST)**, Indio, Cabazon's dinosaurs as black shapes in the dark, Los Angeles (≈10), the 101, the Rincon → **the Anacapa lot ≈12:15 a.m. PST Friday** (Kevin's log: thirty-five hours and change; the way down was 35:40). Go be brilliant." on a stoop with Lena on the next step down, and "cigarettes. and meat on fridays. its a whole system" at midnight — **he loves her and he is leaving her, and every choice in this episode should let the player feel both.** Every option is honest; no option betrays the pact, tells Skye early, or tells anyone but Theo (and Osei, under the seal).

## 2. BEATS — SIX ACTS (scene ids are the Architect's to finalize; ≈ words per path; ♥ = romance material: **J/L** Jack–Lena, **J/S** Jack–Skye)

**Standing canon carried in (S2E16 played):** the tin is **Lena's** — seventeen, in the chest pocket of her jean jacket with the flap buttoned; Jack's breast pocket holds only the RAZR now (the Carhartt still has the worn place). His January pack (opened on St. Brendan's steps in January; six went into the tin) has **eight left**, in his duffel's side pocket — until 1 a.m. Wednesday (s01). Hale's legal pad (THE SECOND LINE; one good sentence at the top; six strips torn off the bottom, the edge ragged). Lena still has his black Bic. Josie has the Bacchus medallion. **Coco** (Laney's Zulu coconut, gold, glittered, purple fleur-de-lis) rides home in the Odyssey's glovebox (Theo objects). Kevin's mother's Odyssey; **WHO DAT** on the back glass. Kevin holds the driver sheet (the glovebox; "RESPECTFULLY — K."). Margot has the Santa Lucia Motor Court Gideon from the drive down. The Broussards' gold bags still hang on the side-yard clothesline, LARGE among them. Celeste left the porch light on.
**The pact (S2E16 s12k), the episode's law, every path:** "And till then, nothing. Nothing either one of us would have to explain. Not to them. Not to Mama. Not in a confessional." — "And you don't kiss me." **No touch — not a hand, not a shoulder, not a coat.** Talk is allowed, and plain. Nobody is told anything but the two best friends, and they only the breakup.

### ACT ONE — "DUST" (Wed Feb 17, 6:20 a.m. to 9:30 a.m.) · ≈4,500 per path

**s01 · WEDNESDAY · 6:20 AM · N. DUPRE STREET, THE ATTIC** · ≈850 · ♥ (J/S text) + Kevin 🔒, Theo · no music (Mémère's EWTN coming up the hatch from the front room — the Ash Wednesday Mass from Alabama, the sound low) · set `{"s2e17_on":true,"lent_day1":true,"resolve.lena":3}`
- **The hangover, recalled in one paragraph (#242: one past-tense backfill, then present):** at one in the morning, home from Bourbon, the porch: Clovis with a bottle of bourbon and a Kool he was made to smoke outside in 1989, the year Tony quit; Remy and Theo; Jack took two fingers and then two more, because his hands needed to hold something that wasn't a Marlboro. **The January pack's last eight went into Clovis's shirt pocket.** Clovis: "That's not how Lent works, cher." — Jack: "It's how mine works." (Clovis kept them. Nobody mentions it again.)
- **Six hours without a cigarette** (the first morning of forty days; he's had six-hour stretches before — this one knows it's forty): his hands. He doesn't know what to do with them; he finds them at his shirt pocket twice before he's awake.
- **The thirteenth morning** of Osei's penance at the gable window, kneeling, the hangover behind his eyes: Mark 6:7–13 from memory; **"shake the dust off your feet"** falls in the middle on Ash Wednesday (one clause; never underlined).
- **Skye, a text from set** (her 4:1x a.m.; on nights in Burnaby): about ashes, in her register, lowercase, no apostrophes, **never prescient** (e.g. *its wednesday where you are. are you dust yet* — the Writer may better it). **CHOICE `s2e17_dust`** (no key, no cue): **[Sincerity]** *“not yet. 8 oclock. ill send proof”* · **[Wit]** *“hungover first. dust at 8”* · **[Candor]** *“yes. since about 1am”* — her one-line reply in register on each (distinct, #251).
- **Kevin** by the hatch, dressed for Mass, the wax 7 in his windbreaker pocket ([`s2e16_kevin=="house"`] — he saved it; 🔒 unglossed; else omit): "Respectfully, you smell like Clovis." **Theo**, a long shape under the Marisol blanket, awake (🔒 he doesn't sleep) — "I'm coming to the ashes. Don't make it a thing." (Theo goes up for ashes; anyone may; never glossed.)
- Exit: Celeste's whisper up the hatch: "Eight o'clock. Coffee's not food."

**s02 · WEDNESDAY · 6:55 AM · DUPRE STREET, THE KITCHEN — COFFEE IS NOT FOOD** · ≈1,000 · ♥ (J/L) + Celeste, Mémère, Remy, the twins, Laney, Theo, Kevin, Margot · no music (the 13-inch dark; the transistor gone to Banks Street with Tony) · set `{"ashwed_kitchen":true,"resolve.lena":3}`
- **The fast-day kitchen:** no grits, no grillades; the Magic Chef cold; a pot of chicory and nothing else on the stove. **Tony left for the market at five** — "Lent is Christmas for a fish market" — and will meet them at church. **Celeste is off today** (she traded a girl in the ICU for Thursday), in a black cardigan, counting cups. **Mémère** at the head in black with a mantilla folded in her lap and her beads — she has fasted every Ash Wednesday since before Truman; Josie, in her school uniform with her bag on: "The Church says you stop at fifty-nine, Mémère." — Mémère in French — Josie: "She says fifty-nine is a number a man made up." The **twins** go at 7:20 to Mass with their class (their school's; fictional, unnamed) and will be home at 2:30.
- **Remy** in sunglasses at the table, gray: announces he is **giving up "brother-in-law" for Lent** ("Forty days. After that, no promises.") — Josie: "That's not a thing you can give up." — "It's a *vice*, Josie." (#199: the bit soured on Monday; now it's renounced.)
- **Laney** hungover in pageant ruins (big sunglasses, a Pi Lam sweatshirt over a black dress, Coco under her arm); **Margot** reading; **Kevin** and **Theo**.
- **Lena** comes down in a black dress with her jean jacket over it (the tin's square in the chest pocket) and her mantilla in her hand. She pours two coffees — his first, as all week, with the milk, without asking — and sets it down **at the far end of the oilcloth**, and takes hers to the other end, and sits there. Nothing about it is said by either of them.
- **The confusion, in reverse (brief "After S2E14", binding; no answers):** yesterday she was inside his coat on St. Charles; this morning there is a table between them. **Josie:** "Did y'all have a fight?" — Lena: "No." — "Then why are you sitting like *that*?" — "It's Lent." — **"Lent's not about *chairs*."** **Mémère**, watching, says something in French — **Josie translates all of it** (#238: no withheld translation here): she says it's the first sensible thing she's seen all week. **Celeste**, over the cups: "Hm." (#235 1 of 2.)
- [`s2e16_kevin=="house"` || `s2e16_kevin=="laney"`] On the counter a Tupperware in Celeste's marker: **KEVIN** — the last of the lemon pound cake, "for Thursday; today you fast." Kevin looks at it and doesn't say anything (🔒 the face, unglossed). [quiet: omit — not this save.]
- **CHOICE `s2e17_chair`** (no key, no cue): **[Sincerity]** *Drink the coffee where she put it.* (he walks the length of the table to it; Lena doesn't look up) · **[Wit]** "It's a long table, Josie." (Josie: "It's the *same* table.") · **[Candor]** "No fight, Josie." (Josie, narrowing: "Then *what*.") — each its own beat (#251).

- The Saturday line in Lent, longer than usual: students, two old women, a man in a Cal Poly jacket; the little light over the confessional door; the card under it (KNOCK (GENTLY) / (AND PROMPTLY) — S2E11's third line, ALL NIGHT TONIGHT — TRAVELERS, gone; #199). The late sun through the west glass.
- **Lena is in the line four ahead of him**, in her jean jacket with the square of the tin in the chest pocket. She doesn't turn around. Then she does — one look, plain — and turns back. **She goes in.** **The seal (🔒, absolute):** the narration says nothing of what happens in there — no length remarked as meaningful, no face read as relief or burden. She comes out; kneels at the side pew by the Marian shrine for her penance; leaves by the side door, passing behind him. At most one word (e.g. "Tomorrow." — Sunday) or nothing (Writer's call; Knowledge Auditor signs it).
- **Jack lets the others go ahead of him**, one after another, until he's the last one and it's 4:44.

**s21 · SATURDAY · 4:45 PM · ST. BRENDAN'S, FATHER OSEI'S OFFICE — TWO CHAIRS** · ≈2,300 · ♥ (J/S and J/L, spoken) + **Osei two-hander** · music **Nana Ampadu & the African Brothers Band — "Ebi Te Yie"** (Osei's turntable, Ghanaian highlife, lifted off as they sit [R: 1967 ✓ credit and date; not in any published field ✓; else E.T. Mensah's "All for You" is spent — use no field]) · set `{"osei_feb20":true,"devotional_confession":true,"absolved_osei_feb20":true,"same_direction_said":true,"wife_line_to_osei":true,"confession_owed":0,"rel.osei.trust":"+2","rel.osei.warmth":"+2","soul.grace":"+2","resolve.jack":"+2"}`
- **§5 IS THE SPEC.** In brief: at 4:45 Osei opens the box door, finds him the last one, and hears that it isn't a confession yet. "Come." The office: **two chairs side by side, facing the crucifix** (S1E21 canon — never pointed out; the staging is the "same direction"); the highlife lifted off; rubber boots by the door; the box of tissues that lives on the desk for this. Jack distraught (not a sin): the plan; **Saint-Exupéry's line from Ma's cross-stitch**; **Nonna's "a good name for a wife"** and that he'd honestly planned to marry Skye someday; Osei won't tell him what to do; talking aloud helps; "To what question?" answered in Jack's fumbling words; the wish from S2E11 named — grief, not sin; **Osei's small laugh: "You have not confessed one thing."**; the venial sins of New Orleans; **devotional confession in Osei's voice**; absolution; penance (an Our Father for Skye Tuesday before he goes up the hill — slowly, for her); and, as a doctor: eat, sleep.
- **CHOICES** (§5.6): `s2e17_osei` (key every option; cue on [Sincerity] "Father Osei heard that.") and `s2e17_last` (no key).

**s22 · SATURDAY · 5:15 PM · ST. BRENDAN'S, THE FRONT STEPS** · ≈400 · ♥ **Romance (J/S)** · no music (through the doors, the 5:00 vigil's opening hymn — "Lord, Who Throughout These Forty Days," public domain; no Gloria in Lent — the Writer must not have one sung) · set `{"vigil_steps":true}`
- The front steps (the pilot's morning-after collision with Lena was on these, at the 11:30 — one clause or none, #199). The first Saturday evening of Lent; the courtyard sprinkler still in pieces; Osei's rubber boots on the step inside the door. He sits. He takes out the pack, turns it once, puts it back over his heart. Tomorrow is Sunday (Lena's; off his page).
- **The RAZR — SKYE (MISFIT):** *3. i made a binder* (S2E16's "a whole system. i want the binder" paid; the Writer may better it; never prescient). **CHOICE `s2e17_three`** (key every option; no cue): **[Sincerity]** *“3”* · **[Wit]** *“send me a page”* · **[Candor]** *“tuesday”* — her reply, one line each (distinct). Keys: *On the steps of St. Brendan's, after confession, Skye texted the count: three. You texted back three.* (variants) → s22a.

**s22a · SATURDAY · 5:20 PM · ST. BRENDAN'S, THE FRONT STEPS** · ≈100 · no music · **`end: true`** (no choice)
- He shuts the phone and puts it back over his heart, next to the pack. Inside, the hymn ends and the readings begin. He sits on the step until the light goes off the Channel. Three days. **End card.**

### THE ROMANCE LADDER, THIS EPISODE (Voice Editor checks it; each step named against the last; **NO TOUCH — and NO KISS — on any path**)
The levee was holding as the whole register; this episode is **the inch**. Every step is a not-touch that costs, and none repeats another's shape (#199):
1. **s02** — two coffees poured, his first as all week; set down at opposite ends of the oilcloth.
2. **s03** — Celeste puts him behind her in the ashes line; Hébert says her name; Jack watches the back of her neck.
3. **s04** — her thumb an inch from his crooked ashes, and back into her pocket, onto the tin.
4. **s05h** (house) — a doorway between two rooms, kept; or **s05m** (market) — none (the cold room is Tony's).
5. **s06** — "Way-back." — the same seat, the opposite reason.
6. **s08** — she hears his half of Skye's call from the way-back; Laney's hand on her knee.
7. **s11** — [Candor] she refuses to take his pack ("You carry it."); [`s2e16_tin=="candor"`] five fingers through the van glass.
8. **s12** — the look across the van roof at Texas Canyon.
9. **s13** — sunflower seeds on his seat (FOR YOUR HANDS).
10. **s14** — "It's Friday." at the Delgado door.
11. **s16** — two glasses of water raised an inch across thirty feet of the Commons.
12. **s17** — the back of the church and the front of it; "One down." on the steps.
13. **s20** — the confession line: four people between them; one look.
(Next touch: S2E19's kiss, after "No." — not before.)

## 2B. BLUEPRINT (Episode Architect — final scene ids, slugs, music, sets, beats, choice maps, inserts) — PRE-PRODUCTION, 2026-10-30, built on the real `saves/after-s2e16.json` (S2E16 played; read flat), the published `episodes/s2e08.json`, `s2e09.json`, `s2e11.json`, `s2e16.json` (every quote below re-checked this pass), the context pack `drafts/s2e17_pack.md`, the period sheet `writers/period/s2e17-feb17-19-2010.md`, `writers/brief.md` "### After S2E07" to the end (read in full this pass) and `tools/validate.py` (its rules bind below)

### 2B.0 Conventions and the Architect's changes (read first)

**Where §2B and anything else in this file differ, §2B wins.** §0–§2, §3, §5, §5a, §6–§6c and §9–§13 are the Showrunner's plan (kept; read §2 through the crosswalk below). **§4.1, §7 and §8 are superseded by §2B** (final as written to `drafts/s2e17_header.json` and the per-chunk briefs `drafts/s2e17_brief_act1.md`–`act5.md`). §5 (the return confession), §5a (the pack), §6 (Skye), §6a (the tellings), §6b (the Broussards) and §6c (hidden canon) bind in full, except where §2B names a correction.

**THE JOURNAL IS RETIRED (player, 2026-10-30).** No `journal` key in any scene, choice or response of S2E17. **(no journal: retired)** stands in every place an earlier blueprint had a journal table. Key lines carry the recap: each is a complete, spoiler-safe sentence in the player's second person, past tense.

**Format of a choice line:** `[Tag] “Text”` (or an italic action line) · **if** gate · **set** `{…}` · **cue** · **key** · → goto · *response gist*. Quoted choice text is on-screen text (writers may polish wording, never meaning). **Every choice's `set` stores its own flag** (`"s2e17_x":"value"`), written out in full below; do not drop it. Anything in `[ … ]` is the writer's brief, not final prose; everything else in quotation marks in §2B is final and is pasted (writers may polish a word for rhythm, never the idea). **Every insert (`sms`, `doc`, `fb`) in §2B is final:** paste it; invent no other insert about either girl.

**Variables.** Schema variables only; `rel.*` only on the tracked ids (`lena theo laney margot kevin osei hale julian evan becca wes skye`) — **never** `rel.celeste / tony / odile / luc / remy / josie / jude / clovis / hebert / gloria / frank / nonna / maria`. Traits and soul `"+1"` (soul.grace `"+2"` once, s21b); `rel.*` ±1 (±2 only where marked; no single scene over ±3 on one axis); `resolve.jack` as `"+N"`/`"-N"` strings; **`resolve.lena` the absolute number `3`** in the scene `set` of s01, s02, s03, s04, s06, s10 only. **No duel this episode** (`"duels": {}` in the header; no `duel.*` var anywhere — the waiting is not a contest; nobody argues anybody into anything).

**Scoring rule (carried from S2E02–E16):** Tag → trait: Sincerity → `trait.sincerity`, Wit → `trait.wit`, Candor → `trait.candor`, Restraint → `trait.control`. **Place and fork tags** (`The market`, `The house`, `Del Playa`, `The stairwell`) pay no trait. **`Faith`** (s03 only) pays `soul.grace` and no trait. `soul.restraint` moves only on choices and scenes about desire or the body — **in S2E17 only at s03 [Restraint], s11 (scene, every path), s11 [Restraint] and s18s (scene)**.

**Engine facts relied on (validator-checked):** a scene `set` applies before the body renders and before choice `if`s are evaluated; a choice's `set` applies before its response renders; `next` cannot read flags; **one choice set per scene, at the scene's end — nothing in the body may follow a choice**; a scene has `choices` **or** `next` **or** `end` (the `end` scene carries no choice); **every scene with choices has at least one option with no `if`**; response items may be strings, inserts or `{"if","text","else"}` objects; inserts may carry `if`; string flags are truthy when set; unknown vars read 0/false; `&&` binds tighter than `||`, **no parentheses**; resolve clamps 0–10. **A body paragraph cannot set state, and a scene `set` cannot be conditional** — branch-dependent flags are set on the choice where the branch is decided.

**Assembly** refuses duplicate scene ids across drafting files — **every scene id below belongs to exactly one drafting chunk (§2B.8).**

**Conditionals.** (a) **S2E16 and everything earlier is PLAYED**: read flat; every earlier-episode read is still written defensively (if/else on the real flag name; the `if` branch is the played path) — §2B.4 lists every one. (b) **S2E17's own choices** gate later S2E17 lines; every such gate is exhaustive (`=="a"` · `=="b"` · `!="a" && !="b"`) or an `if`/`else` pair, **never nested**. (c) The Wednesday fork is read on the string **`s2e17_wed`** (`market` / `house`); the Saturday-night fork on **`s2e17_night`** (`playa` / `stairs`).

**Texts and clocks.** `{"type":"sms","from":"…","text":"…","time":"…"}`. **The RAZR follows the towers:** Central through Wednesday and Thursday morning; Mountain from past Van Horn (Thu ≈9:30 a.m. local); Pacific from the Colorado River (Thu ≈6:40 p.m.). The `time` on every insert is Jack's local time. Skye is on nights in Burnaby (Pacific) all week; the narration does the time-zone arithmetic **once**, at s01 (#201). **Skye counts by her own working days** (her Friday-night shift is still "4" at 2:14 a.m. Saturday; Saturday evening is "3"). Senders (labels as published): **SKYE (MISFIT)** (lowercase, no apostrophes), **Jack** (lowercase T9, short, no apostrophes — except where he answers his father in his father's capitals, s04), **Dad** for Frank (ALL CAPS). Lena's LG shows **JULIAN** on its outside screen once (s08a) — the name only; his voice is never heard. **No typing indicators, read receipts, front cameras, "selfie," emoji, "viral," "trending," "Instagram," "DM," "text thread." No lyric quoted from any needle-drop, hymn or chant beyond the public-domain first lines named here; titles only.**

**Real people:** Olympians (Lindsey Vonn, Shaun White, Evan Lysacek), Tiger Woods, any newsreader — **no words**, mentions only, on a screen through glass or with the sound off. No Austin plane crash on the page (heavy news; nobody needs it). Nobody knows anything after Sat Feb 20.

**Ages (all adults):** Jack 18, Lena 18, Theo 19, Laney 19, Kevin 19, Margot 19, Skye 19, Remy 20, Luc 24. **Jude and Josie are 13** — a question each, a translation, a score kept; nothing near them but that. Alcohol: Clovis's porch bourbon (recalled, 1 a.m., adults); nothing else on the page.

Whatever y’all aren’t saying to each other — keep not saying it." — **s07's, s07 [Wit]'s and s16's lines carry no "Respectfully"**, change 9). **Remy's "brother-in-law" once** (s02, in the renouncing). **Celeste's "Hm." twice** (s02, s06a — #235's two). **Withheld translation once** (s05h, Lena's half — house path only; #238). **Margot's field note one** (s12, #30). **"Lord have mercy" (Lena, sincere) ≤1** (none planned). **"Sweet suffering Saint Sebastian" ≤1** (s13, optional, at the satsumas). **Lena's watched exit once** (s13, to the store — #27). **Lena's laughs with sound: none.** **"In Youngstown…": none** (#236 unspent; s07 [Wit] says "Ohio"). **Knocking wood, "jinx": none** (#237 retired). **Averted-eyes chorus ≤3** (#232: s06 Luc, s07 the visor mirror, s16 Laney). **"A hand's width" once at most** (s14, #50). **"Since [waypoint]" ≤4** (#222/#231). **Past-tense backfill openers ≤2** (#242: s01's porch, s10's five o'clock). **Time-zone arithmetic once** (s01). **Jaw / throat / breath once each in the episode** (#3/#4). **Chest-hand gestures ≤6 across branches** (#246 — the pack over his heart counts: s11a, s18s, s22, s22a are the planned four). **"Like a man/woman" similes ≤3** (#243). **Transit codas ≤3** (#70 — the van exits are a sign, a clock, a line of dialogue; never "the van goes on west"). **River furniture: the Basin (s08) and the Colorado (s13), one line each** (#247). **Crowd surge as the engine for touch: none** (#240 — there is no touch).

**Architect's changes to the Showrunner's rev. 1, and why:**
1. **One choice set per scene** (validator) → splits per the crosswalk: s04/s04a, s06/s06a, s08/s08a, s11/s11a, s13/s13a, s21/s21a/s21b. No pasted tails: every shared beat after a choice lives in the following scene, or inside each response in different words (#251).
2. **Previously is six lines per path** (brief, standing: "Keep 'Previously on' light (4–6 lines)"; rev. 1 asked ten). Kept rev. 1's lines **1, 2 (triple), 3, 7, 8, 9**. Cut: 4 (the tin — paid on the page at s11a), 5 (Skye's stoop — paid at s08), 6 (the Lent text — paid at s08/s22), 10 (Wes — optional in s21a). Final in §2B.9 and the header.
3. **The withdrawal is the habit's, not nicotine's (continuity, binding).** Jack has lit nothing since **No. 17, the El Paso air pump, 11:55 p.m. Feb 5** (`jack_cigs` 17 / `jack_cigs_jf` 6 — six from the January pack in six weeks; none in New Orleans). There is no nicotine left in him to withdraw from. What the brief's "withdrawal, itchy hands" means on this save is the *other* withdrawal — a habit told no for forty days with no exit, in the worst week of his life, on no sleep and, Friday, no food: the hands at the empty pocket, the itch under the skin, the short fuse, the fog, the want of the ritual (the cellophane, the flick, the first drag) that every fight since August ended in. It is written frankly and physically (plan §5a) — but **nobody on the page calls it chemistry**. Kevin's diagnosis (s10) is recast to be medically true: **“It’s not the nicotine. You smoke like it’s a holy day of obligation. It’s the hands — the habit’s been told no. Textbook. Tired and hungry makes it worse. Respectfully, you’re a hazard to navigation.”** (rev. 1's "peaks at forty-eight to seventy-two hours" was true of a pack-a-day man, not of this one). s01's "six hours without a cigarette" becomes the first morning of the vow (the body let go of him somewhere in Louisiana; the hands haven't). The days stay counted by Lent: day two (Thu), day three (Fri), day four (Sat).
4. **Music: 4 per path on both forks.** s05h gets **Mahalia Jackson — “Move On Up a Little Higher”** too — Mémère's record on the front-room console after EWTN's noon Angelus (Mahalia was born Uptown; it's Mémère's Lenten record), so the house path isn't a three-cue path. Same record both branches (S2E16's precedent).
5. **Keys: 12 per path at most** (schema 6–12; rev. 1 listed 14 scenes): **s03, s04 (Sincerity and Candor only), s06, s07, s08, s09, s11, s12, s13, s18p|s18s, s21, s22** — 12 on every path but the s04 [Wit] path (11). **No key** on s05m|s05h and s15 (rev. 1's own trim order). Cues: **6 in the file, ≤6 per path** (§2B.3).
6. **The Saturday clock (canon: St. Brendan's 5:00 vigil, bible §8.5/continuity S1E14; Osei celebrates it).** Rev. 1 put Jack in Osei's office at 4:45 for ≈25 minutes, which runs into the vigil Osei must say. Fixed: the line opens at 3:30; Lena goes in at 3:40; **Jack lets the others go ahead until he's the last one at 4:14; Osei opens the box door at 4:15 and says "Come."** The office 4:15–≈4:52 (Osei: "twenty minutes in that chair" lands ≈4:35); he takes off the purple stole for the vigil's and goes to vest; **s22 at 5:00 on the front steps (the opening hymn through the doors); s22a at 5:10 — Jack goes in at the back as the readings begin (none quoted)** instead of sitting out the vigil on its steps (rev. 1). A man just absolved goes to Mass; Sunday stays S2E18's. **The brief's "regular confession hours" holds** (he goes in during them).
7. **s12 reordered** so the choice answers the beat before it: the silent film → Laney back to the van — Theo opens his mouth — "Don't." — "Don't you *dare*." → Kevin → Margot's note turned round for Jack → Lena back from the boulders; the look across the van roof → **Laney crosses to Jack's table last and sets Coco down: "Hold her. With your *life*."** → `s2e17_coco`.
8. **The route clock, re-run against the map (≈2,006 miles; Jack's "Two thousand and six," s17):** depart Dupre ≈3:15 p.m. CST Wed → the Mississippi at Baton Rouge ≈4:30 → **the Basin bridge at dusk ≈5:45** (sunset ≈5:58) → Houston ≈9:15 → San Antonio ≈12:30 a.m. (Jack takes the wheel) → past Sonora ≈2:40 → **Fort Stockton ≈4:55 a.m. — Kevin's sheet: "SLEEP 2 HRS. EVERYBODY."** (rev. 1 had the van at Fort Stockton at sunrise, which the miles don't allow without a stop) → sunrise ≈7:40 CST (s10) → past Van Horn, Mountain time → **El Paso ≈10:20 MST** (s11) → **Texas Canyon ≈2:15 p.m.** (s12) → Tucson → Phoenix's Broadway Curve ≈5:30 MST → the Colorado River, Pacific time → **Blythe ≈6:45 p.m. PST** (s13) → Indio → Cabazon → Los Angeles ≈10:30 → the 101 → the Rincon → **the Anacapa lot ≈12:30 a.m. PST Fri** (s14). Kevin's log: **“Thirty-five fifteen.”** (the way down was 35:40 — "Twenty-five minutes faster. Nobody fought.").
9. **Kevin's "Respectfully" capped at three** (s01, s10, s12): s07 “Bread and water’s not the minimum, Lena.” · s07 [Wit] “That’s not true, and you know it’s not true.” · s16 “Hypoglycemic and withdrawing. You shouldn’t operate a stapler.”
10. **s15 reordered:** the mail he carried up at one in the morning (Nonna's card) and the call to Ma (Celeste's "call your mother," paid — no longer optional) come **before** he kneels at the window; the penance's end is the scene's last beat and its choice.
11. **s16 reordered:** Laney looks at both trays, gets up and leaves; Theo follows "for napkins"; Margot's dry line; **then** Lena, alone at the window table, lifts her glass an inch → `s2e17_bread`.
12. **The ashes formula.** The 1985 US Sacramentary's text is "Remember, man, you are dust and to dust you will return"; **Hébert, ordained before the Council, says it the way he has said it for fifty years: “Remember that you are dust, and to dust you will return.”** (the player's words). The narration never comments on the wording.
13. **The Del Playa [Wit] text** no longer depends on Santa Barbara weather (UNV): *“its not raining here. im jealous”*.
14. **s19's Candor line** answers Gloria's read of his face instead of repeating "Church. Three-thirty." (cut from the body); **s19's Sincerity option** is paired on `s2e14_gloria` (the Saturdays kept / given to Hector).
15. **Gated option pairs** so every option is true on every save (each pair: exactly one shows): s06 [Wit] on `s2e16_mon` (Destiny's sister exists only on the king-cake path); s21 [Candor] on `skye_knows_wife_line`; s19 [Sincerity] on `s2e14_gloria`; s22 [Wit] on `s2e16_lent`.
16. **Tony's 1:30.** On both forks Tony eats the one meal at home at half past one, goes back to Banks Street for the afternoon, and is at the curb at three (s06a) — rev. 1 had him at the house at 1:30 and "in from the market at three" without the trip between.
17. **Lena's confession (s20) is silent on her side**: one look going in, nothing coming out; no word (rev. 1's optional "Tomorrow." is not used — nothing that ties her time in the box to the plan; the seal, both ways).
18. **Five acts, not six.** The act headings in §2B follow the five drafting chunks (ACT ONE "DUST" s01–s04a · ACT TWO "SEND-OFF" s05m–s07 · ACT THREE "THE OTHER WAY" s08–s11a · ACT FOUR "HOME" s12–s16 · ACT FIVE "THE SICK" s17–s22a); rev. 1's six act titles survive as the plan's beat groupings in §2 only. Nothing in the episode file shows an act.
19. **No resolve gate anywhere; no dangerous-night gate exists** (resolve.jack touches 2 at s17a on the lowest path — nothing reads it); no sex for Jack or Lena regardless (hard rule). **No touch between Jack and Lena in any line of any scene** — the Voice Editor flags any stage direction that drifts (a coat handed, a shoulder brushed in a doorway, a seed bag passed hand to hand — none).

### 2B.2 Period guardrails specific to this blueprint (the prose leans only on the safe wording given here)
- **Calendar.** Wed Feb 17 = **Ash Wednesday** (Lent begins; Easter Apr 4); Thu Feb 18; **Fri Feb 19 = the first Friday of Lent**; Sat Feb 20 (the vigil anticipates the First Sunday of Lent — its readings OUT as scene content). Thin waxing crescent low in the west after sunset Wed–Fri (new moon Feb 14) — once at most (s08 or s12's dusk), never a beat.
- **Liturgy (hedged; never preached; at most a clause in Jack's head, never underlined).** Ash Wednesday: Joel 2:12–18 ("Rend your hearts, not your garments"), Ps 51, 2 Cor 5:20–6:2, **Matthew 6:1–6, 16–18** ("wash your face, so that you may not appear to be fasting"). **The distribution formula as Hébert says it** (change 12): “Remember that you are dust, and to dust you will return.” Thu: Deut 30:15–20 / Luke 9:22–25 · Fri: Isa 58:1–9a / Mt 9:14–15 · Sat: Isa 58:9b–14 / **Luke 5:27–32** — the end-card tag only: “Those who are healthy do not need a physician, but the sick do.” [R: NAB wording]. **Ash Wednesday is not a holy day of obligation** (the church is fuller than any Sunday anyway). **Lent:** purple; no Gloria, no Alleluia; no flowers; subdued organ. **Fast and abstinence (USCCB):** Ash Wednesday fast for 18–59 (one full meal, two small that together don't equal it) and abstinence from meat for 14+; Fridays of Lent abstinence. Jack and Lena (18) are bound; Mémère (88) is not and fasts anyway; Theo, Laney, Margot are not bound by anything they acknowledge. **Bread and water on Fridays is a private devotion beyond the rule** (Kevin's point, s07).
- **Penance (Osei, S2E11 [Candor], played):** Mark 6:7–13 "every morning until you are home" — Wed the **thirteenth** morning (s01; "shake the dust off your feet," Mark 6:11, one clause), Thu the **fourteenth** (s10, Margot's Gideon), Fri home (s15, `s2e17_home`); Saturday Osei doesn't ask, and it isn't needed.
- **Confession (s20–s21b):** St. Brendan's Saturday confessions 3:30–4:45 (canon); the box with the screen and the card under the light (KNOCK (GENTLY) / (AND PROMPTLY) — S2E11's third line gone); **face-to-face in the office is canonical** (the penitent chooses; Jack chooses by sitting down); the absolution formula exactly (§5.2 beat 12 — ICEL 1974); **venial sins need not be confessed and are encouraged** (CCC 1458's sense, never cited by number); temptation is not sin; feelings are not sins; ending a non-marital relationship honestly is not a sin. **The 5:00 vigil** follows; Osei celebrates it (change 6).
- **New Orleans, Ash Wednesday (s01–s06a):** the Quarter swept; ashes on foreheads at work and on the streetcar; Masses early (the eight o'clock at Holy Rosary, the family's); Broussard's open at six, a line out the door ("Lent is Christmas for a fish market"); **weather cool, damp, overcast in the morning, breath visible at dawn (UNV — never a beat beyond breath and coats)**; a clearing, colder dry day for the drive (UNV, hedged). EWTN's Ash Wednesday Mass from Alabama on Mémère's TV [plausible]; the Angelus at noon on EWTN [plausible].
- **The road (s07–s14):** I-10 west (change 8's clock). **The Atchafalaya Basin bridge** — the long elevated causeway over the swamp, cypress, no heron this time; **Houston** a gas stop off I-10 (unnamed); **past Sonora**, a picnic area with concrete tables and a windbreak (plausible — never named); **Fort Stockton** a truck stop off I-10 (unnamed); **Van Horn**, then Mountain time; **the El Paso station** (S2E12's, fictional: PAY INSIDE AFTER 10 PM still taped over the card reader; the air pump at the edge of the lot; Juárez across the channel); **Texas Canyon rest area** (the granite boulders stacked like somebody's dropped game; ramadas; wind); **Phoenix's Broadway Curve** at rush hour; **the Colorado River at Blythe**, the clocks to Pacific; **the California agricultural inspection station on westbound I-10 at Blythe** ("Any fruits or vegetables?" — Celeste's satsumas surrendered; **Coco passes**: the inspector hefts it, sees it's drained, dried and painted, and calls it a craft — safe either way, and this is the way it goes); a Chevron in Blythe; Indio; the San Gorgonio windmills' red lights; **Cabazon's dinosaurs** as black shapes in the dark; Los Angeles at ten-thirty; the 101; the Rincon. **Westbound I-10 has no Sierra Blanca Border Patrol stop** (the checkpoint stops eastbound traffic) — so there is none on the page. A pack of Marlboro Reds in El Paso: "a five and change" (UNV — never a number). Gas ≈$2.60–2.70 (UNV — no number).
- **News/sports on the page (mentions only, no quotes, through glass or sound off):** Shaun White's halfpipe gold Wed night (s08a, a truck-stop TV in Houston — UNV day, hedge "the snowboarder"); Lindsey Vonn's downhill gold (s10's TV at Fort Stockton, the replay — hedge "a skier, gold"); Lysacek (optional, s16's Commons TV, or nothing); **Tiger Woods's statement Fri morning** (optional at s16, one clause, the sound off, no words, no editorial — Remy's poster is the only rhyme, in Jack's head). OSU lost to Purdue Wed night (Frank does **not** text it this episode — he is a man of one word this week). **Never:** the Austin plane crash, the Chile quake, the Gulf, anything after Feb 20, any invented words for any real person.
- **Marisol (s14–s22a):** fog and the cold off the Channel at 12:30 a.m. Friday; St. Brendan's Lenten Stations **Fridays at 7:00 p.m.** (new canon); the Commons at noon; Del Playa at two in the morning (a party going out like a tide; a couch on a lawn); the east stairwell (the push bar, the fluorescent hum); the Anchor's Saturday fishermen shift 7–1 (the Saturdays kept, S2E14); the courtyard sprinkler still in pieces. **Weather Fri–Sat UNV** — no rain beat (change 13); the Saturday light going gold on the west glass at four is safe (sunset ≈5:50).

### ACT ONE — "DUST" (Wed Feb 17, 6:20 a.m. to 9:20 a.m.) · ≈4,600 per path (chunk 1)

#### s01 · `WEDNESDAY · 6:20 AM · N. DUPRE STREET, THE ATTIC` · ≈850 · **chunk 1 ♥ (Jack/Skye text) + Kevin 🔒, Theo** · cold open · no music (Mémère's EWTN coming up the hatch from the front room — the Ash Wednesday Mass from Alabama, the sound low) · set `{"s2e17_on":true,"lent_day1":true,"resolve.lena":3}` · choice `s2e17_dust` (no key, no cue) · *are you dust yet*
- **Opener (no smell opener — #71):** gray at the gable window; the roofing nails over his face; Remy's Tiger poster; a hangover behind his eyes like a hand pressing; EWTN up the hatch, a choir he can't make out.
- **The porch at one in the morning — ONE past-tense paragraph, then present (#242, 1 of 2):** home from Bourbon after the horses; the porch; **Clovis** with a bottle of bourbon and a Kool he has smoked outside since 1989, the year Tony quit and made him take them out; **Remy** and **Theo** on the steps; Jack took two fingers in a jelly glass, and then two more, because his hands needed to hold something that wasn't a Marlboro. **The January pack's last eight went into Clovis's shirt pocket.** Clovis: **“That’s not how Lent works, cher.”** — Jack: **“It’s how mine works.”** Clovis kept them. (Nobody mentions it again; Celeste did not see; Lena was in bed.)
- **The hands (change 3):** he hasn't lit one since the air pump in El Paso twelve days ago; whatever the body wanted, it gave up somewhere in Louisiana. The hands haven't. **Twice before he's properly awake he finds them at his shirt pocket**, where there's nothing, and where the tin used to be, and that's two different nothings. Forty days. The first morning of a thing knows how long it is.
- **The thirteenth morning:** he kneels at the gable window with the hangover behind his eyes and says Mark 6:7–13 from memory, word-perfect; **“shake the dust off your feet”** falls in the middle of it on Ash Wednesday (one clause; never underlined; no gloss).
- **Kevin** by the hatch, dressed for Mass (windbreaker, khakis, his hair wet-combed): **“Respectfully, you smell like Clovis.”** (Respectfully 1 of 3.) Gate `s2e16_kevin` (§2B.4): **[house]** he takes the wax 7 out of his windbreaker pocket, looks at it, and puts it back (🔒 unglossed; Jack draws no inference). **Theo**, a long shape under the Marisol blanket, eyes open (🔒 he doesn't sleep — never glossed): **“I’m coming to the ashes. Don’t make it a thing.”** (Theo goes up for ashes; anyone may; the narration doesn't explain why he wants to.)
- **Celeste's whisper up the hatch:** **“Eight o’clock. Coffee’s not food.”**
- **The RAZR on the sill** — her 4:14, wrapping a night on the Burnaby lot (**the time-zone arithmetic, once in the episode — #201**): paste `{"type":"sms","from":"SKYE (MISFIT)","text":"its wednesday where you are. are you dust yet","time":"6:14 AM"}`

#### s02 · `WEDNESDAY · 6:55 AM · DUPRE STREET, THE KITCHEN — COFFEE IS NOT FOOD` · ≈1,000 · **chunk 1 ♥ (Jack/Lena) + Celeste, Mémère, Remy, the twins, Laney, Theo, Kevin, Margot** · no music (the 13-inch dark; the transistor gone to Banks Street with Tony) · set `{"ashwed_kitchen":true,"resolve.lena":3}` · choice `s2e17_chair` (no key, no cue) · *opposite ends*
- **The fast-day kitchen:** no grits, no grillades; the Magic Chef cold; a pot of chicory on one burner and nothing on the others. **Tony left for the market at five** (Celeste, counting cups: “Lent is Christmas for a fish market. He’ll meet us at church.”). **Celeste is off today** (she traded a girl in the ICU for Thursday), in a black cardigan, counting cups and not counting people. **Mémère** at the head of the table in black with a mantilla folded in her lap and her beads; she has fasted every Ash Wednesday since before Truman. **Josie**, in her school uniform with her bag already on: **“The Church says you stop at fifty-nine, Mémère.”** — Mémère, in French, at some length — Josie: **“She says fifty-nine is a number a man made up.”** **The twins** leave at 7:20 to go to Mass with their class and will be home at 2:30 (Celeste says so, as a law).
- **Remy** in sunglasses at the table, gray: **“I’m giving up ‘brother-in-law’ for Lent. Forty days. After that, no promises.”** — Josie: **“That’s not a thing you can give up.”** — **“It’s a *vice*, Josie.”** (#199: the bit soured on Monday; now it's renounced. The word is said once, here, in the renouncing — counted.)
- **Laney** hungover in pageant ruins (big sunglasses, a Pi Lam sweatshirt over a black dress, Coco under her arm like a clutch); **Margot** reading a paperback Durkheim, the only person in the room who slept; **Kevin** with his hands folded; **Theo** with a coffee he isn't drinking.

Today you fast.” Kevin looks at it and doesn't say anything (🔒 the face, unglossed). **[laney]** the same, in Laney's silver Sharpie with a crown over the V. **[quiet]** nothing.
- **Lena** comes down at seven in a black dress with her jean jacket over it — the square of the tin in the chest pocket under the buttoned flap — and her mantilla in her hand. **She pours two coffees, his first, as all week — the chicory, the milk, without asking how** — and sets his down **at the far end of the oilcloth**, and takes hers to the other end, and sits there. Nothing about it is said by either of them. (The ladder's first rung; #249: never labeled.)
- **The confusion, in reverse (brief "After S2E14", binding; no answers):** yesterday she was inside his coat on St. Charles; this morning there's a table between them. **Josie:** **“Did y’all have a fight?”** — Lena: **“No.”** — **“Then why are you sitting like *that*?”** — **“It’s Lent.”** — **“Lent’s not about *chairs*.”** **Mémère**, watching both ends, says something in French — **Josie translates all of it** (#238: no withheld translation here): **“She says it’s the first sensible thing she’s seen all week.”** **Celeste**, over the cups, not looking at either of them: **“Hm.”** (#235, 1 of 2.)

#### s03 · `WEDNESDAY · 8:00 AM · OUR LADY OF THE HOLY ROSARY, ESPLANADE AVENUE — DUST` · ≈1,550 · **chunk 1 ♥ Romance (Jack/Lena) + Hébert, Tony, Celeste, Mémère, Remy, Laney, Theo, Kevin, Margot, the twins (with their class)** · no music field (a cantor alone, a Lenten chant, unaccompanied — no title quoted) · set `{"ashes_holy_rosary":true,"ashes_hebert":true,"soul.grace":"+1","resolve.jack":"+1","resolve.lena":3}` · **key every option; no cue** · *the man who poured the water*
- **The walk (one sentence):** Esplanade under the oaks with beads still in them, Mémère on Remy's arm because he needs hers more.
- **The eight o'clock on Ash Wednesday:** the church fuller than any Sunday (it isn't a holy day of obligation and nobody here cares); purple; nothing on the altar but the cross and a glass bowl of ash; **the two Broussard pews on the Gospel side, Mémère on the aisle**; **the twins up front with their class** in uniforms (Josie turns around once to check on the family, and Jude turns her back around). **Tony** comes in late from the market in his green canvas jacket with ice on the cuffs and the case still on him, kneels at the end of the pew, and **prays aloud the way he does** — one line, e.g. **“Lord. It’s Lent. You know what that means for my business. Thank You.”** (the Writer may add a half-line about "the boy," or not). **Theo** at the far end of the second pew, very still (🔒). **Laney** with her sunglasses pushed up into her hair. **Margot** "observing" (she'll go up: “Participant observation.”). **Kevin** kneels like he means it.
- **The readings — Jack's hangover hears one line of each, at most** (Joel 2: “rend your hearts, not your garments”; Matthew 6: “wash your face”). **Hébert's homily — one sentence** (Breaux Bridge accent; seventy-four; short as always): **“Yesterday this whole city put on a mask. Today He puts a little dirt on your face so you can’t hide behind it — and then the Gospel tells you to go wash your face and not make a show. You will wear it out that door anyway. Wear it humble.”**
- **The ashes:** the family goes up in family order; **Celeste puts Jack behind Lena** in the line without a word (the boyfriend's place, one last time — nobody comments; he watches the back of her neck under the mantilla). **Hébert, who poured the water at her baptism**, puts his thumb in the bowl and makes the cross on her forehead: **“Remember that you are dust, and to dust you will return.”** (change 12) — and then, low, only to her: **“Magdalena.”** (Pays E16 nextTime.) She says **“Amen.”** Then Jack: the formula; the thumb, cold and gritty; **“Le garçon de l’Ohio.”** A crumb of ash falls onto his eyelash and stays.
- **Back in the pew, kneeling**, dust on his face and a hangover behind it: **what he offers his Lent for.** On Royal Street he told Skye, “You give it to Somebody. It’s a present.” Now he has to choose what it's wrapped around. Gate `s2e16_lent` (§2B.4): **[sincerity]** one clause — he told her at midnight he'd say one for her in the morning; he does, first. **[else]** nothing.

#### s05m · [market] `WEDNESDAY · 9:40 AM → 1:30 PM · BROUSSARD’S SEAFOOD, BANKS STREET — CHRISTMAS FOR A FISH MARKET` · ≈1,400 · **chunk 2 Tony two-hander + Mr. Alcide, Miss Earline, the Lent line** · music **Mahalia Jackson — “Move On Up a Little Higher”** (the red radio over the oyster bench, WWOZ's Ash Wednesday gospel hour) · set `{"tony_ashwed":true,"worked_case_ashwed":true}` · choice `s2e17_ice` (**no key** — change 5; no cue) · *to your face*
- **The line out the door at 9:40 on Ash Wednesday**: half of Mid-City with ashes on their foreheads buying for Friday — shrimp by the pound, catfish, crawfish for the weekend, a man buying forty pounds of drum for a church fish fry; **Miss Earline** in her rain bonnet for "a pound and the news"; **Mr. Alcide** at the oyster bench singing in French over Mahalia; **Tony at the register with a smudge on his forehead and the phone in his neck**. Jack on the case: **Gloria's first rule** ("nothing sits in its own water") is a law of the Gulf (S2E14) — the ice banked high at the back, the melt running for the drain. Three and a half hours that don't let him think, and he's grateful. **His hands have a job.**
- **1:15 p.m., the walk-in:** Tony shuts the door. Their breath. **He does not ask again** — blunt about not asking. Gate `s2e16_mon` (§2B.4): **[cake]** **“Monday I asked you a question at the rail on Spanish Plaza in front of half of Mid-City.”** / **[else] “Monday I asked you a question in this cooler.”** — then, both: **“I’m not asking it in my cooler.”** — Jack: **“I’d answer it if I could, Mr. Broussard.”** — **“I know you would. That’s why I’m not asking.”** Then the blunt thing, **from what he's seen** (observation, not knowledge — Knowledge Auditor signs): somebody on a phone is going to get bad news; maybe two somebodies; **“Whoever it is — you tell them to their face. Broussards do it to your face.”** And **a card** out of his apron, written on the back in carpenter's pencil against the cooler wall — paste `{"type":"doc","title":"A business card from Broussard’s, the back written on in carpenter’s pencil","text":"BROUSSARD’S SEAFOOD · BANKS STREET · NEW ORLEANS\n—\nFOR GLORIA. GULF SHRIMP, BROUSSARD PRICE. ASK FOR TONY."}` — **“Who taught you ice? Gloria. You give her that. Bring her.”** (S2E14's "Bring her." paid forward.)

#### s10 · `THURSDAY · 7:40 AM · FORT STOCKTON, A TRUCK STOP OFF I-10 — THE FOURTEENTH MORNING` · ≈850 · **chunk 3 Kevin, Margot, Theo, Laney (Lena at the edge)** · no music (a truck-stop TV through the glass) · set `{"penance_14":true,"rel.margot.warmth":"+1","resolve.jack":"-1","resolve.lena":3}` · choice `s2e17_snap` (no key, no cue) · *a hazard to navigation*

- **The fourteenth morning:** **Margot hands him the Gideon without looking up** (SANTA LUCIA MOTOR COURT stamped on the edge — S2E12 s17; #199) — he doesn't need it; he says Mark 6 from memory leaning on the van in the cold, the dust in it again; he hands it back; she takes it without looking up either. (Thursday's Gospel — "take up his cross daily" — never preached, never quoted at him.)
- **The habit, told no — day two, written frankly (change 3):** his hands (the label off a bottle of water in strips; knuckles; drumming on the roof rack); the itch under the skin of his forearms; the short fuse; the fog in his head that the coffee doesn't lift; the want of the ritual — the cellophane, the flick, the first drag — so exact he can taste it. **Kevin's diagnosis** (sunny scalpel; medically true): **“It’s not the nicotine. You smoke like it’s a holy day of obligation. It’s the hands — the habit’s been told no. Textbook. Tired and hungry makes it worse. Respectfully, you’re a hazard to navigation.”** (Respectfully 2 of 3.) **Theo** says one harmless thing (e.g. that Coco is technically a drupe), and **Jack snaps at him** — hard, unfair, out of nowhere (e.g. that nobody in this van has ever once cared about a single thing Theo's said that wasn't about Theo) — the episode's first venial sin; Saturday he'll confess it. Lena, at the edge of it with a coffee by the van's back bumper, looks at the ground.
- **Hale's legal pad:** to give his hands a job he starts the essay under the one good sentence at the top of THE SECOND LINE, on his knee, the bottom of the pad ragged where the six strips came off (S2E16 s06). Gate `s2e16_line` (§2B.4): **[reckless]** one italic line of the second paragraph — the trumpet pointed at him off the curb · **[else]** the second line going by from the curb. By El Paso, two pages.

Then you told him you’d meant to marry her someday.”** → s21a · *Osei waits as long as it takes (he has sat up with a great many people at this hour). The clock on the shelf. Then Jack says it plainly — someday; the chair; Nonna's line — the whole thing in four sentences, like a man reading a chart he's had for a while.*

#### s21a · `SATURDAY · 4:25 PM · FATHER OSEI’S OFFICE — THE SAME DIRECTION` · ≈950 · **chunk 5 ♥ (Jack/Skye and Jack/Lena, spoken) + Osei** · no music field (the record off) · set `{"same_direction_said":true,"wife_line_to_osei":true,"rel.osei.warmth":"+1"}` · choice `s2e17_last` (no key, no cue) · *you have not confessed one thing* — **§5.2 beats 5–10 bind**
- **Opener — 🔒 the widower hears "a good name for a wife"** (once, here, on every path): Osei is quiet a long time; his hand rests on the bad knee; he takes his glasses off and puts them on again. **Nothing glossed. Ever.** (Abena, Esi and Kwame stay §8.5's private truth.)
- **Beat 5 — "same direction":** Jack tries to say why, and can't, and then says it with somebody else's words — **Saint-Exupéry, from the cross-stitch over the phone in his mother's kitchen on Glenwood Avenue** (a wedding present; he grew up reading it while he waited for the phone to ring): ***Love does not consist in gazing at each other, but in looking outward together in the same direction.*** Then his own words, fumbling (the Writer's): Skye looks at him — the best thing that ever looked at him; she went to Mass to understand him, she knelt, she walked at the March — and **Lena looks where he's looking.** It doesn't make Skye less. It makes it worse. **Osei** may say one pilot's or physician's sentence (the man who wrote it crashed in the Sahara; he knew something about direction) — **he does not endorse a choice.** Optional gate `s2e16_wes` (§2B.4): **[candor]** ≤40 words — the man Monday who said don't wait; "Now I'm waiting four days." — Osei: “Waiting is not the same as waiting *on*.” / [else] nothing.
- **Beat 6 — "To what question?":** Osei's own January line (S2E08; in the Previously). Jack answers it now, in his words — something like **the question was which way I'm facing** — and is not sure it's the right answer, and says so. Osei, no verdict: **“Then you have one more question than you had in January. That is progress, in my old profession.”**
- **Beat 7 — the wish, named:** gate `s2e11_sorry` (§2B.4): **[candor] “On the morning you left, I told you the part of you that wished she had not stopped was not sorrow yet — to bring it to me when you could name it.”** / [sincerity] / [restraint] / [else] (the variants in §2B.4). Jack names what's there now — not that wish anymore; this one: **that it could have been both; that he didn't have to hurt her to tell the truth.** Osei: **that has a name. It is grief. Grief is not a sin. I cannot absolve it. You carry it — and He carries it with you.** (Sound: the Church absolves sins, not sorrows.)
- **Beat 8 — Skye, honored:** gate `osei_blessed_odyssey && skye_blessed` (§2B.4): **[if] “I put water in her hair at a quarter to five in the morning. She shut her eyes. I remember that.”** / [else] “I met her once, at the van, in the dark. I remember that she came.” Gate `s2e8_noon` (§2B.4): **[candor]** he does not say "I told you so" about "if you make her the answer you make her the occasion" — if he refers to it at all, it's to say **she was never only an occasion; she was a person, and Jack loved her as one, and that is why this costs** (brief standing: love wills the good of the other — the real good in it) / [else] nothing. **Never a thesis; never "almost the thing"; never a cautionary tale.**
- **Beat 9 — the small laugh (brief, binding):** **“Jack. You have been in that chair twenty minutes, and you have not confessed one thing.”** — Osei laughs, small, from the belly; and Jack, surprised, laughs too, once (#101 — vary; a laugh into nothing).
- **Beat 10 — the venial sins of New Orleans (no mortal sin exists — `confession_owed` 0):** Jack examines himself out loud and finds the small things, honestly — **the fixed list, all paths, the Writer's words**: **(a)** Clovis's bourbon at one in the morning on Ash Wednesday — so he wouldn't want a cigarette; in the first hour of Lent (intemperance, not drunkenness); **(b)** snapping at Theo on Thursday for nothing — gate `s2e17_snap` (§2B.4): [sincerity] “I said sorry. It was still mine.” / [wit] “I didn’t say sorry to him. I said it to a coconut.” / [else] “I never said sorry. I wrote instead.”; **(c)** fasting Fridays partly to beat her — gate `fridays_said` (§2B.4): [if] “I said it out loud in the van so she’d hear it.” / [else] “I didn’t tell anybody. I still wanted her to find out.” — Osei: **“Fasting out of pride is still fasting. The pride you can leave with me.”**; **(d)** an uncharitable hope about Julian — that he'd be unkind to her on Sunday, so Jack wouldn't have to go on admiring him (small, real, venial; the Writer may find its truer shape). Then the last one (the body ends here):
- **CHOICE `s2e17_last`** (no key, no cue; every option → s21b):
  - [Candor] **“And I bought a pack of cigarettes. I haven’t opened it.”** · set `{"s2e17_last":"pack","trait.candor":"+1"}` → s21b · *Osei: **“That is not a sin. That is a very expensive rosary.”** — and a physician's half-sentence: keep it in the pocket, and keep the hand off it, and come back if the hand wins.*
  - [Sincerity] **“And on the levee I held her inside my coat, before we stopped. I wanted her there.”** · set `{"s2e17_last":"coat","trait.sincerity":"+1"}` → s21b · *Osei, soundly and briefly: to want is not to consent; temptation is not sin; he stopped. If there was a fault, it was standing where the wanting had no wall — an imprudence, small, and he has confessed it; the truth on Tuesday is its repair, and that is his, not Osei's. **“God does not usually waste a trip.”***
  - [Wit] **“And I ate two pralines out of a tin that was supposed to go back full, and moved the rest around so it wouldn’t show.”** · set `{"s2e17_last":"tin","trait.wit":"+1"}` → s21b · *Osei laughs again, from the belly, longer. **“That one I will absolve twice.”** (Gloria said nine; the tin looked full; s19 stands.)*

#### s21b · `SATURDAY · 4:45 PM · FATHER OSEI’S OFFICE — ƐYƐ` · ≈550 · **chunk 5 Osei** · no music field (**the needle back on the record only as Jack goes out** — the same record; no new field) · set `{"devotional_confession":true,"absolved_osei_feb20":true,"confession_owed":0,"soul.grace":"+2","resolve.jack":"+2","rel.osei.trust":"+1","rel.osei.warmth":"+1"}` · `next: s22` · *it is well* — **§5.2 beats 11–14 bind**
- **Beat 11 — devotional confession, in Osei's voice** (brief; CCC 1458's sense, never cited by number; **two or three sentences, one physician's line; never a lecture; never the term "devotional confession" without his own explanation**): you were not required to bring any of this — the Church asks only for mortal sins — but she is glad when you bring the small ones; a doctor likes to see the healthy patient too, it is how he learns what healthy looks like; small cuts bleed a long time if nobody looks at them; **it teaches your conscience its own handwriting**; it lets Christ heal what you would have called nothing.
- **Beat 12 — contrition and absolution:** Jack says the Act of Contrition from memory (the one Ma taught him; the Writer's call of wording — the traditional "O my God, I am heartily sorry…"). **Osei's hand raised over him, no screen; the absolution exactly** (Rite of Penance, ICEL 1974): ***“God, the Father of mercies, through the death and resurrection of his Son has reconciled the world to himself and sent the Holy Spirit among us for the forgiveness of sins; through the ministry of the Church may God give you pardon and peace, and I absolve you from your sins in the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit.”*** — **“Amen.”** (Whole or in part; nothing implies grace was earned.)
- **Beat 13 — the penance:** **an Our Father for Skye, on Tuesday, before he goes up the hill — “Slowly. For her. Not for you.”** Gate `s2e17_ashes` (§2B.4): **[skye]** Jack: “I offered Lent for her. On Wednesday.” — Osei: “Then Tuesday’s Our Father is a bead on the same string.” / [else] nothing. Then, taking off the stole — as a doctor, not a confessor: **“Did you eat today?”** — **“Gloria made me.”** — **“Then she has done my job. Go and sleep. A man should not tell the truth on four hours’ sleep; it comes out crooked.”** (One "I'm good" allowed in the scene only if Osei catches it: “You say ‘good’ the way my patients said ‘fine.’” — §5.5; optional.) **“Ɛyɛ.”** (It is well — the one Twi word; untranslated or glossed once in Osei's mouth, the Writer's call.)
- **Beat 14 — out:** Osei hangs the stole for the vigil's and goes to vest (the five o'clock is his); the needle back on the record as Jack goes out; the sacristy hall; the vigil bell. **Osei never:** tells him what to do; weighs the two women; mentions anything he holds under the seal by so much as a pause; promises Tuesday will go well; jokes about Skye; preaches Saint-Exupéry; calls anything a mortal sin (§5.3).

Osei holds Lena's and Theo's confessions under the seal and the narration never brushes them; Jack's confession is everything since New Orleans and nothing of Danny's night (§15 — the narration never claims "nothing left to confess" as a total).

True and hard (§4.5); never a lecture; Skye's curiosity honored.
